Motivation: 6D姿态估计在现实应用中需要准确、快速和可扩展，特别对未见过的物体。WAPR旨在改进这一问题。

Method: WAPR使用旋转对称先验校准姿态目标，为旋转对称物体提供宽视角支持。它通过减少无信息的大误差情况的影响，采用角度平衡损失来稳定学习。并构建了具有这种先验的大规模6D训练数据集SA6D。

Result: 实验结果显示，WAPR在七个BOP核心数据集上实现了未见过物体的6D姿态定位和检测的最佳性能，在快速且无约束的推理设置下也不例外。

Conclusion: WAPR通过角度平衡损失和旋转对称先验，在实时准确的6D姿态估计方面取得了显著进展，对于现实中的实时应用具有很大的潜力和意义。

Abstract: Real-world applications require 6D pose estimation to be accurate, fast, and scalable to unseen objects. This paper introduces WAPR, a zero-shot wide-angle pose refinement model that refines candidate poses with rotational deviations up to 90 degrees. With as few as 12 candidate poses per detected object instance, WAPR supports fast inference within 1 s per frame and reaches a pose-estimation throughput of up to 25 detected object instances per second. To support wide-angle training for rotationally symmetric objects, WAPR uses rotational symmetry priors to canonicalize symmetry-equivalent pose targets before loss computation. We further construct SA6D, a large-scale 6D training dataset with such priors. SA6D obtains KASAL-assisted rotational symmetry priors for 944 GSO scans and expands them through geometry and texture augmentation into about 50K augmented object instances and about 2M rendered RGB-D images. In addition, an angle-balanced loss stabilizes learning across different angular ranges by reducing the influence of uninformative large-error cases. Experiments on seven BOP core datasets show that WAPR achieves state-of-the-art performance in unseen-object 6D pose localization and detection under both fast and unconstrained inference settings. Project page: https://github.com/WangYuLin-SEU/WAPR.

</details>


### [73] [Visual Evidence Under Cross-Examination: Evaluating and Controlling Decision-Level Evidence Use in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09550)
*Huiyao Zhang,Jin Bai,Zilong Su,Rui Guo,Chaofan Qin,Jinze Lv,Wenhui Yu,Hongfei Wang,Ye Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了CROSS-Bench基准，包含28,000个决策问题，并通过RIVET界面增强了视觉证据的有效性转移，从而提高了模型在Vision-Language模型任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的Vision-Language模型在利用视觉证据时存在局限性，即有效的视觉证据可能会对支持它的候选对象产生影响，而不直接对其有益。因此，研究者引入了CROSS-Bench基准，并设计了RIVET界面来解决这一问题。

Method: 研究者开发了CROSS-Bench基准，并设计了RIVET界面，该界面能够保持证据身份和不确定性，结合候选条件生成响应，并单独控制响应强度。实验中，研究者进行了共享证据实验，展示了任务准确率和证据所有权之间的差异，并通过共轭证据实验进一步证明了RIVET的有效性。

Result: 在RIVET的辅助下，模型的标准化效应转移量从0.512增加到0.651，尤其是在预测证据来自原始输入的情况下，四个冻结的骨干网络在CROSS-Bench上的平均准确率提高了5.70个点。

Conclusion: 研究证明了视觉证据的有效性转移对于提高Vision-Language模型的整体性能至关重要。RIVET界面能够解决当前模型中存在的问题，使其更有效地利用视觉证据，并在多个任务上都表现出了优势。

Abstract: Vision-language models increasingly reason through crops, regions, and tool-produced observations. Yet an observation can influence the answer without benefiting the candidate it supports. We study candidate-bound visual contribution: valid evidence should help, invalidating its supporting relation should remove its additional effect, and valid rebinding should redirect that effect to the newly supported candidate. We introduce CROSS-Bench, a benchmark of 28,000 decision problems, with matched invalidation and rebinding tests on a dedicated evaluation subset. Our RIVET interface preserves evidence identity and uncertainty, composes a candidate-conditioned response, and separately controls its strength. Shared-evidence experiments show that task accuracy and evidence ownership can diverge. Under matched capacity and training, RIVET increases normalized effect transfer from 0.512 to 0.651 where clean evidence has a positive effect. The advantage persists on common evaluation examples and across repeated decision-layer fits. With evidence predicted from raw inputs, RIVET improves CROSS-Bench accuracy by an average of 5.70 pp across four frozen backbones, relative to the same models without auxiliary evidence. These results separate the utility of visual evidence from the candidate-specific destination of its effect.

</details>


### [74] [Gradient-Based Trajectory Optimisation over Continuous Poses for Sparse-View Cone-Beam CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09579)
*Linda-Sophie Schneider,Simon Wittl,Gabriel Herl,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过将每次源位置视为独立连续变量，并在整个源位置上进行协同梯度上升，优化了锥形束CT的轨迹。优化目标包括平面覆盖、连续视场协变损失以及带束衰减意识的射线。该方法处理了圆周路径、有限C臂、双轴和自由球参数化。该方法在需要更密集扫描时表现最佳，且在整个扫描包络内有效。优化器还实现了将预扫描设计与计划的扫描轨道匹配，消除了物体特定的重建基的需求。该方法在机器人CT平台上进行了验证，尽管未显示相对于均匀带采样的一致度量增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态候选池方法无法进行离轴细化，并需要针对每次数据采集几何体进行特定的对象预计算，这会极大增加计算成本和时间。本文通过提出一个动态的连续源位置优化框架，解决了这个问题。

Method: 研究方法中，每隔两次扫描就增加一次新的源位置，并将这些位置连续优化。通过在扫描器的运动学流形上进行梯度上升，找到最优路径。优化目标是平衡不同采样方法的覆盖效果和成本效率。

Result: 这种优化方法首次能够在一个通用框架中处理多种CT几何结构，包括圆形轨道、有限C臂、双轴和自由球参数化。在Defrise流管上测试时，连续选择可以恢复圆形轨道无法发现的层缺陷，即使是在更稀疏的预算下也能达到自由球的性能，只需一个小的扫描倾斜角度就能获得大部分的好处。

Conclusion: 连续的CT轨迹优化能够在不依赖物体特定基的情况下实现预扫描计划，从而大大减少计算时间和资源消耗。该方法在机器人CT平台上进行了初步验证，但在实际应用中仍需进一步测试和评估。

Abstract: Trajectory optimisation for cone-beam computed tomography (CT) determines which information sparse-view scans acquire. Fixed candidate pools prevent off-grid refinement and require new object-specific precomputation for each acquisition manifold. We make every source pose an individual continuous variable and move all poses jointly by gradient ascent on the scanner's kinematic manifold. The objective combines soft-Tuy plane coverage, continuous View Covariance Loss, and an analytic attenuation-aware ray-bundle penalty. The same optimiser handles circular, limited C-arm, two-axis, and freesphere parametrisations. On a Defrise flange, continuous selection recovers laminar defects invisible to a circular orbit, matches discrete swap search on the free sphere at the sparser budget, and leads at the denser one, with the same objective evaluated in every arm. A moderate elevation band already recovers most of the free-sphere gain at the defects, so the same optimiser transfers to bounded scanner envelopes. Photon noise preserves the ordering on the flange and compresses it on a dense fuel nozzle. Sparseprescan planning benefits from matching prescan and planned acquisition manifolds. Selection takes seconds rather than minutes without an object-specific reconstruction basis. Prescan-planned poses were executed on a robot CT bench and reconstructed in a common frame, demonstrating feasibility but no consistent metric gain over uniform band sampling. Continuous pose optimisation incorporates attenuation and scanner constraints directly into sparse-view acquisition design.

</details>


### [75] [STORK: Spatio-Temporal Observation of uterine contRactions via neural networKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09598)
*Melissa Schween,Tristan Gottwald,Jordina Aviles Verdera,Lisa Story,Mary Rutherford,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于胎儿MRI中子宫收缩活动检测的弱监督学习方法STORK，该方法在大约700个系列的数据上达到了95.0%的AUROC和94.6%的AUPRC，显著优于基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前子宫收缩活动通常在MRI图像中手动识别并丢弃，导致难以深入了解收缩动力学。为了克服这一限制，研究提出了STORK模型以实现自动检测。

Method: STORK模型采用多实例学习方法，基于动态MRI系列的粗略系列级别标签进行训练。它通过将三维时空卷积分解为平行分支来捕捉相干组织运动，而无需使用完整的四维卷积。模型利用帧级嵌入合并强度和Demons估计的位移场，并通过线性平均池化头进行聚合，从而可以在不需要帧级训练监督的情况下恢复帧级收缩评分。

Result: 在约700个多供应商动态胎儿MRI系列上，STORK实现了系列级别的AUROC 95.0%和AUPRC 94.6%，显著优于基准模型（3D ResNet和ConvNeXt）。

Conclusion: GRAD-CAM分析表明，该模型利用预测特征扩展超出胎盘到子宫组织，提供了一种自动化的工具，用于更丰富的子宫行为表型分析。

Abstract: Uterine contractions in fetal MRI are typically identified manually and discarded, limiting insights into contraction dynamics. We formalize Uterine Contractile Activity Detection (UCAD) as a weakly-supervised learning problem and introduce STORK, a multi-instance learning model trained on dynamic MRI series using only coarse, series-level labels. STORK factorizes 3D spatio-temporal convolutions into parallel branches across temporal hyperplanes to capture coherent tissue motion without the cost of full 4D convolutions. Per-frame embeddings, combining intensity and Demons-estimated displacement fields, are aggregated by a linear mean-pooling head. This ensures that frame-level contraction scores can be recovered post-hoc without frame-level training supervision. Evaluated on around 700 multi-vendor dynamic fetal MRI series, STORK achieves a series-level AUROC of 95.0% and AUPRC of 94.6%, substantially outperforming 3D ResNet and ConvNeXt baselines. Grad-CAM analysis suggests that the model draws on predictive features extending beyond the placenta into the uterine tissue, offering an automated tool for richer phenotyping of uterine behavior.

</details>


### [76] [Identity-Duplication Auditing in National-Scale Neuroimaging Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09614)
*Jiheng Li,Michael E. Kim,Trent M. Schwartz,Yuhan Cui,Gaurav Rudravaram,Derek B. Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Lori L. Beason-Held,Victoria L. Morgan,Dario J. Englot,Angela L. Jefferson,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,for the BIOCARD Study team,for the Health,Aging Brain Study,:,Health Disparities,Study Team,Lianrui Zuo,Guray Erus,Christos Davatzikos,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAPPEN是一个结合SHA-256指纹检测精确重复和监督对比检索可能来自同一人的不同扫描的人工审查流程，用于审计T1加权脑MRI数据中的身份重复问题。该研究在包含95,129个扫描的大规模汇集中部署了这一工具，并成功识别了1,316组精确重复扫描和1,275组支持的人近重复主体组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的MRI数据汇编方法可能导致同一个个体出现多个身份标识，这可能泄露训练和测试数据，导致下游生物医学研究中表面上的性能过拟合。因此，需要一个能够大规模审计这种问题的全流程工具。

Method: HAPPEN采用SHA-256指纹检测精确重复，并结合监督对比检索可能来自同一人的不同扫描。被检索出的扫描对在本地托管的界面中由人工审查，而不是自动分类为重复。

Result: HAPPEN在95,129个扫描的大规模MRI数据中部署，成功识别了1,316组精确重复扫描和1,275组支持的人近重复主体组。所有54个遗传参考对都被成功恢复。

Conclusion: HAPPEN能够在不重新训练模型或传输图像的情况下进行部署，并成功审计了大规模MRI数据集中的身份重复问题。同时，外部机构也独立完成了全流程工作，验证了工具的可移植性。

Abstract: National-scale magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) repositories increasingly integrate data from different studies and institutions. However, subject identifiers that are valid only within individual datasets are no longer guaranteed to remain globally unique after aggregation, making it possible for the same subject to be assigned multiple identifiers, which we define as identity duplication. Such duplication can create leakage between training and test data and inflate apparent performance in downstream biomedical studies. Existing methods do not provide an end-to-end, image-based workflow for auditing this problem at repository scale. In this work, we present HAPPEN, a human-in-the-loop pipeline for auditing identity duplication in T1-weighted brain MRI repositories. It combines SHA-256 fingerprinting for exact-duplicate detection with supervised contrastive retrieval of non-identical scans that may originate from the same person. Retrieved pairs are reviewed as candidates in a locally hosted interface rather than automatically classified as duplicates. We deployed the workflow in a 95,129-scan aggregated repository and assessed end-to-end recovery using 54 genetic-reference pairs. Transferability was assessed by locally deploying the same workflow on 22,386 scans at an independent institution without model retraining or image transfer. Deployment in the study repository identified 1,316 exact-duplicate scan groups and 1,275 reviewer-supported near-duplicate subject groups. Of these groups, 56% and 82%, respectively, crossed dataset boundaries. All 54 genetic-reference pairs were recovered. The external team independently completed the full workflow using a locally selected operating threshold and review standard.

</details>


### [77] [Quasi-Binarized Autoencoders: An Architecture-Independent Information Bottleneck for Medical Image Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09670)
*Shouhei Hanaoka,Takahiro Nakao,Atsushi Takamatsu,Takeharu Yoshikawa,Osamu Abe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过引入分立化层（QB层），本文提出了一种新的无监督异常检测模型QBAE，该模型在网络中每个编码器-解码器路径上无遗漏地应用了该层，从而在MedIAnomaly基准上的多个图像级AUROC均超过0.828，特别是在BraTS2021数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型依赖于数据集特定的架构设定来阻止网络学习恒等映射，而这些设定无法以信息量度来表述。本文旨在提供一种统一的方法，确保在任意架构中都限制信息流，并且每份重构的图像都满足隐私保护的界限。

Method: 引入了分立化层（QB层），该层通过将每个潜在元素压缩到[0,1]区间并添加Laplace噪声以提供局部ε-差分隐私，从而限制了模型传递给解码器的信息量。

Result: 在MedIAnomaly基准的七个数据集上，QBAE达到了0.828的平均图像级AUROC，这是未针对每个数据集进行调整的方法中最高纪录，在BraTS2021数据集上的表现尤为突出，AUROC为0.911，像素级平均精度（AP）达到0.838。

Conclusion: QBAE模型通过在全球性的每次编码器-解码器路径上应用分立化层，确保了异常检测的有效性，并且通过这项研究，该方法为未来的研究提供了新的理论基础和实践路径。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection, which learns only from normal images, is a central task in medical image analysis and remains an open problem. Reconstruction-based methods pass an image through an encoder-decoder network trained on normal data and detect anomalies from the residual between the image and its reconstruction. This works only if the information passed from the encoder to the decoder is limited; otherwise the network learns an identity mapping and reconstructs anomalies too. This limit is usually imposed through architectural choices, tuned per dataset, that cannot be stated in bits. We introduce the quasi-binarizing (QB) layer, which squashes each latent element into [0, 1] and adds Laplace noise of scale 1/epsilon. Each element is then epsilon-locally differentially private, and the mutual information between an image and its reconstruction is bounded by a quantity that depends only on epsilon and the number of QB elements, whatever the encoder and decoder. Placing a QB layer on every encoder-decoder path, including all skip connections, we build QBAE, a seven-level attention U-Net with 32,768 QB elements. On the seven datasets of the MedIAnomaly benchmark, QBAE with one architecture and one configuration reaches a mean image-level AUROC of 0.828, the highest among methods that do not adapt to each dataset, and the best reported results on BraTS2021 (AUROC 0.911, pixel-level AP 0.838). The noise is kept at test time, so that every reconstruction satisfies the bound. Without input corruption, the bottleneck alone prevents identity collapse (mean AUROC 0.805 vs. 0.590). Code is available at https://github.com/hanaokalog/MedIAnomalyQB.

</details>


### [78] [A Multi-Source Ultrasound Benchmark Revealing the Limits of Contemporary Self-Supervised Anomaly Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09677)
*Marco Riedenauer,Daniel Kienzle,Pratik Mayekar,Rainer Lienhart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究引入了SADUSI基准数据集，旨在评估多种解剖区域、视角及成像协议的异常检测方法。研究发现现有的自监督异常检测方法在该基准上的表现不佳，尤其是重建扩散方法，这表明跨源的超声异常检测仍然是一个亟待解决的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于医学超声中异常图像的获取可能依赖于详细的病理标注，这些标注往往难以获得。因此，研究提出使用自监督异常检测方法来直接从正常图像中学习异常信号，特别是在面对多源数据时。为了评估这类方法，需要一个能够涵盖不同解剖区域、视角及成像协议的数据集。

Method: 该研究构建了一个多源超声数据集SADUSI，用于训练和评估异常检测方法。数据集中包含了多种解剖区域的图像，并且使用了有代表性的自监督异常检测方法进行评估。

Result: 代表性的自监督异常检测方法在SADUSI上的表现不尽如人意，尤其是基于重建扩散的方法（如AnoDDPM和DeCo-Diff）在像素级别的AUROC值为0.56-0.72，最高F1分数为0.10-0.26，表示难以区分病理与正常图像区域。特征基于的PatchCore变体表现稍好，达到像素级别AUROC值0.76-0.83，但最高F1分数仅为0.14-0.40。

Conclusion: 当前的自监督异常检测方法在跨源超声数据环境中的表现较差，尤其是对于病理和正常图像区域的精确区分。这揭示了在多源超声异常检测方面的挑战仍需进一步解决，同时SADUSI数据集可用于开发能够在不同解剖特定设置之外泛化的异常检测方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised anomaly detection is a promising paradigm for medical ultrasound, as normal images are often easier to obtain than exhaustive annotations of all possible pathologies. However, most existing evaluations are limited to a single anatomy or task, making it unclear whether models learn a robust notion of normal ultrasound appearance or only a source-specific representation. We introduce the SADUSI benchmark, a multi-source ultrasound dataset designed to train and evaluate anomaly detection methods across a broad range of anatomical regions, views, and acquisition protocols. The goal of SADUSI is to provide a diverse normal ultrasound distribution and a benchmark for visible structural anomalies that can be assessed from single images. We evaluate representative self-supervised anomaly detection methods and find that current approaches struggle in this setting. In particular, reconstruction-based diffusion methods such as AnoDDPM and DeCo-Diff achieve pixel-level AUROC values of 0.56-0.72 and maximum F1 scores of 0.10-0.26, indicating limited separation of pathology from normal image regions. Feature-based PatchCore variants perform better, reaching pixel-level AUROC values of 0.76-0.83, but remain limited with maximum F1 scores of 0.14-0.40. These findings suggest that broad multi-source ultrasound anomaly detection remains an open challenge and that SADUSI can serve as a resource for developing methods that generalize beyond anatomy-specific settings.

</details>


### [79] [PARC-Loc: Text-to-Point-Cloud Localization with Partial Assignment and Relational Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09761)
*Shengkai Ma,Zhenyu Hou,Weihua Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PARC-Loc 提出了一种基于 Partial Assignment with Relational Consistency 的粗到细定位框架，通过建模提示对象的兼容性和成对的空间关系，解决了现有方法中的布局不一致别名和边界证据不完整问题，提高了在 KITTI360Pose 和 CityLoc 数据集上的定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗到细方法在处理重复或相似的都市物体导致检索子地图嵌入相似性增加，以及查询相关信息跨越子地图边界导致检索子地图缺乏上下文证据的问题时表现不佳。PARC-Loc 的目标是解决这些问题，提高局部化精度。

Method: PARC-Loc 通过联合建模提示对象的兼容性和成对的空间关系，允许匹配元素的同时倾向于与查询布局一致性的情况。在粗阶段，使用候选级评估补充神经相似性来选择布局一致的子地图；在细阶段，通过周围相关实例扩展上下文，同时 PARC 产生对象级别的匹配权重，以引导跨模态注意力。

Result: PARC-Loc 在 KITTI360Pose 和 CityLoc 数据集上表现出色，优于传统粗到细基准方法。在 KITTI360Pose 上，Top-1 定位召回率从 0.50 提高到 0.67，相对于最强基线实现了 34% 的相对增益。

Conclusion: PARC-Loc 通过其提出的机制在城市大规模 3D 地图中实现更准确的文本到点云局部化，展示了其在复杂场景中的优越性。

Abstract: Text-to-point-cloud localization estimates a position in a city-scale 3D map from descriptions of surrounding objects. Existing coarse-to-fine methods retrieve submaps using aggregate learned compatibility and then localize within a selected submap. However, repetitive or similar urban objects can inflate the embedding similarity between the query and multiple submaps, even when the instance layout within a submap violates the query description. Meanwhile, query-relevant instances often span submap boundaries, leaving the retrieved submap with incomplete contextual evidence. We term these failure modes layout-inconsistent aliasing and boundary evidence incompleteness, respectively. To address them, we propose PARC-Loc, a coarse-to-fine localization framework built on Partial Assignment with Relational Consistency (PARC). PARC jointly models hint-object compatibility and pairwise spatial relations, allowing unmatched elements while favoring assignments consistent with the queried layout. At the coarse stage, its candidate-level assessment complements neural similarity for layout-consistent submap selection. At the fine stage, the context is expanded with query-relevant instances from adjacent submaps, while PARC yields object-level matching weights that guide cross-modal attention. Extensive experiments on KITTI360Pose and CityLoc show that PARC-Loc outperforms conventional coarse-to-fine baselines. On KITTI360Pose, our method improves Top-1 localization recall at 5 m from 0.50 to 0.67, achieving a 34% relative gain over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [80] [What Makes Synthetic Hard Negatives Work in Vision-Language Pretraining?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09700)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Andreas Floros,Kleanthis Marios Papadopoulos,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了将合成难样本从单模态自监督学习转移到多模态预训练中存在的问题，并提出了一种新方法SNAP来解决这些问题。SNAP通过构造模内难样本避免了两种失败模式，并且在多种架构和数据集上展示了对检索、分类和线性探针评估的一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管合成难样本在单模态自监督学习中取得了成功，但将其应用到多模态预训练中面临挑战，因此研究需要分析问题并提出改进方法。

Method: 研究首先分析了六种多模态表示空间合成策略的缺点，识别了导致效果不佳的两种主要失败模式。然后，通过几何学分析提出了SNAP，该方法生成模内难样本，不涉及模态间的正样本，从而避免了上述两种失败模式。SNAP方法模型无关，无需外部生成模型，并且增加不到10%的训练时间。

Result: SNAP在CLIP和FLIP等架构上进行了评估，在零样本检索、零样本分类和线性探针评估中都实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究提出了SNAP方法，通过构造模内的难样本解决了多模态预训练中合成难样本问题，实现了性能的改善，为多模态预训练领域的研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Synthetic hard negatives generated in the representation space have proven effective for unimodal self-supervised learning, but transferring this idea to vision-language pretraining is not straightforward. We analyze six representation-space synthesis strategies and identify two failure modes in their transfer to vision-language pretraining: cross-modal constructions that produce overly easy negatives or pull them toward the query, and intra-modal constructions that incorporate the matched positive. We also observe logit-scale saturation when training with synthetic hard negatives and a learnable temperature, and find that fixing the temperature improves downstream performance. Using this geometric analysis we propose SNAP, which generates intra-modal hard negatives that never involve the positive from either modality, avoiding both failure modes entirely. SNAP is model-agnostic, requires no external generative models, and adds less than 10% training time overhead. Evaluated on top of CLIP and FLIP across multiple architectures and datasets, SNAP delivers consistent improvements on zero-shot retrieval, zero-shot classification, and linear probe evaluation.

</details>


### [81] [Latent Watermarks under Generative Editing: A Benchmark and Analysis of Detection Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09702)
*Sung Ju Lee,Nam Ik Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，编辑处理对水印检测的影响显著，而附加的扰动则能够揭示更广泛的水印检测生存范围，同时提出了一种新的水印检测生存诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估编辑过程对水印检测的影响，并探索在生成模型上应用编辑的有效方法，从而确保水印的安全性和有效性。

Method: 研究对八种水印方法在五种编辑器上的表现进行了基准测试，涉及四种生成模型、五种编辑强度和四类语义类别。实验步骤包括编辑有效性和阈值检查，并揭示了编辑过程对四种生成模型和不同语义类别的水印检测的影响。

Result: 研究结果显示，编辑过程对不同生成模型的 Tree-Ring 类型水印检测产生了主要区分作用，而添加的扰动则揭示了更广泛的水印检测生存范围。连续编辑揭示了第二类隐藏差异：分数分离度下降而检测率接近天花板。水印检测生存情况可由标准化清洁分数分离度($d'$)来组织，而空间重叠在预测清洁可检测性之外对未来编辑生存情况的预测几乎没有帮助。研究还发现，在 HSTR 方法中，对齐角布局对比在匹配清洁分离度时无法解决。

Conclusion: 这项研究提出了一个新的水印检测生存诊断方法，并表明标准化清洁分数分离度($d'$)是此基准中的一个有用的诊断指标，对未知方法的适用性则参差不齐。研究成果将用于代码和辅助材料的独立发布。

Abstract: Ordinary prompt-based editing can cause latent watermark detection to fail without explicitly targeting the watermark. We benchmark eight watermark methods against five editors across four generative backbones, four editing strengths, and five semantic categories, with edit-validity and threshold checks. Separating editing from seven subsequent distortions reveals that editing alone primarily distinguishes Tree-Ring, while added distortions expose a broader spectrum of detection survival. Sequential edits reveal a second hidden difference: score separation can decline while detection rates remain near their ceiling. Across methods, standardized clean score separation ($d'$) organizes composite-survival tiers, whereas spatial overlap adds little to predicting edit-only survival beyond clean detectability. Embedding-strength interventions in two methods link higher clean separation to higher post-edit separation. In HSTR, the margin contrast is positive, while the angular layout contrast at matched clean separation remains unresolved. Together, outcome decomposition and continuous separation expose differences hidden by aggregate TPR. Method tiers are stable under threshold recalibration at the main operating points and alternative composite weights. Clean $d'$ is thus a useful empirical diagnostic within this benchmark, with mixed transfer to unseen methods. Code and supporting artifacts are planned for a separate release.

</details>


### [82] [Enhancing Multi-Region Stylization with Interior-Guided Boundary Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09706)
*Hong-Son Nguyen,Thi-Ngoc-Hanh Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Interior-Guided Boundary Repair（IGBR）的方法，用于在多区域风格迁移时修复边界问题，能够防止对象间的风格泄漏并保持内部特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于区域的神经风格迁移方法在组合多个区域时容易出现边界失真问题，影响视觉效果，因此提出IGBR解决这一问题。

Method: IGBR通过内部引导传播来修复边界像素，并应用受限于对象-背景边界的基于距离的内向融合，避免了对象间的风格泄漏。该方法基于区域约束的闭式解形成，无需重新训练即可集成到现有的风格迁移流程中。

Result: 该研究引入了量化评估方法以衡量边界一致性、梯度伪影、对象间泄漏和内部保留。实验结果显示，IGBR在边界一致性、梯度稳定性及内部保留等方面优于先前的融合技术。

Conclusion: IGBR为多区域风格迁移提供了一种有效的方法，能够生成更合理的边界，并能无缝集成现有风格迁移管道中，未来可进一步研究其在实际应用场景中的性能。

Abstract: Region-based neural style transfer enables fine-grained artistic control by allowing independent stylization of semantic image regions. However, compositing these regions often leads to boundary artifacts, degrading visual quality. We propose Interior-Guided Boundary Repair (IGBR), a lightweight and model-agnostic method that improves boundary handling in multi-region stylization. IGBR repairs boundary pixels using interior-guided propagation and applies inward, distance-based blending restricted to object-background boundaries, preventing inter-object style leakage. The method is derived from a region-wise constrained formulation with a closed-form solution and can be seamlessly integrated into existing stylization pipelines without retraining. To evaluate efficiency of our IGBR, we introduce quantitative metrics that measure boundary consistency, gradient artifacts, inter-object leakage, and interior preservation without requiring annotated stylized images. Our experiments and evaluations demonstrate that the proposed IGBR consistently produces plausible boundaries, outperforming prior blending techniques in boundary consistency, gradient stability, and interior preservation. The code is available at https://github.com/Son-SDT/IGBR.

</details>


### [83] [DynStream: Online Streaming 4D Gaussian Reconstruction of Dynamic Worlds from Unposed Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09720)
*Dingwei Xian,Xiaoyu Zhou,Yajiao Xiong,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynStream是一个框架，可以从不间断的视频流中在线重建动态4D场景，无需针对每个场景进行优化，从而实现高保真度的在线动态重建和渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法在同时实现连续处理和高保真渲染方面存在困难，DynStream旨在解决这个问题。

Method: DynStream通过在局部时间窗口内重建场景并逐步对齐和融合这些局部重建，以全局一致的方式生成场景。这种方法联合实现了跨窗口几何一致性和动态场景内容的建模，以支持高效的重建和高保真的渲染。

Result: 实验表明，DynStream能够在长时间的视频流中实现高质量的在线动态重建和渲染，并在多种动态的室内和室外场景中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: DynStream为在线动态4D场景重建提供了一种有效的解决方案，该方案能够在长时间视频流中实现高保真度的实时重建和渲染。

Abstract: Online reconstruction of dynamic 4D scenes from long, unposed streaming videos requires both continuous processing and photorealistic rendering, which existing methods struggle to achieve simultaneously. Existing feed-forward Gaussian methods are restricted to offline processing, whereas online point-cloud approaches struggle to maintain dense geometry and high-fidelity rendering. We present DynStream, a framework for streaming 4D Gaussian reconstruction from long, unposed videos. Given a continuous video stream, DynStream reconstructs the scene within local temporal windows and incrementally aligns and fuses these local reconstructions into a globally consistent scene, enabling online 4D reconstruction without per-scene optimization. By jointly enforcing cross-window geometric consistency and modeling time-varying scene content, DynStream supports efficient reconstruction and photorealistic rendering over extended video streams. Experiments demonstrate that DynStream enables high-fidelity online dynamic reconstruction and rendering from long video streams, achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse dynamic indoor and outdoor scenes.

</details>


### [84] [MeshCarve: Artisan Mesh Generation with Flow Matching in Compact Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09723)
*Xiyu Wang,Ruocheng Wu,Yufei Wang,Zhihao Li,Lanqing Guo,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshCarve 是一种通过层次稀疏变换器骨干网络将顶点位置和边连接分开生成的方法，缩短了标记序列，同时保持了重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的artisan网格生成方法大都采用自回归预测方式，这导致了推理速度慢的问题。而流匹配方法虽然可以处理连续潜空间，但当几何和拓扑一起编码并且进一步压缩潜空间时，重建质量显著下降。因此，作者提出了MeshCarve方法，通过层次稀疏变换器骨干网络分别生成顶点位置和边连接，并在较小的潜空间中进行流匹配。

Method: MeshCarve 使用层次稀疏变换器骨干网络，包括顶点VAE和边VAE，分别编码顶点位置和边连接，并通过一种称为顶点连接编码的机制将任意顶点连接转化为固定长度的连续顶点嵌入，从而保持复杂的艺术拓扑结构。MeshCarve 还引入了一种锚生成器，并进行流匹配。

Result: MeshCarve 在Objaverse和Toys4K上优于现有的自回归和流匹配方法，在较小的标记序列下保持了高质量的重建。

Conclusion: MeshCarve 是一种创新的方法，它在生成阶段全部运行在空间压缩的潜空间中，显著降低了标记序列长度，并展示了在不牺牲重建质量的情况下实现高效网格生成的能力。

Abstract: Prior artisan mesh generation works largely predict face tokens autoregressively, which makes inference slow. Recent methods instead flow match continuous latents built by Variational AutoEncoders (VAEs), but reconstruction quality drops significantly when geometry and topology are jointly encoded, and further when the latent space is compressed. We present MeshCarve, a flow matching method that generates entirely in compact latent spaces, generating vertex positions and edge connections separately and sidestepping the difficulty of a joint compact latent. To shorten the token sequence, we propose a hierarchical sparse transformer backbone, instantiated as VertexVAE and EdgeVAE. Instead of encoding fields over the surface voxels, both VAEs anchor on discrete vertices in their latent spaces, which drastically reduces the token sequence length, and our spatial-aware compression shortens it further without costing reconstruction. VertexVAE directly encodes vertex occupancy. For connectivity, we propose vertex-link encoding, which turns arbitrary connectivity between vertices into fixed-length continuous per-vertex embeddings and recovers complex artistic topology faithfully. MeshCarve combines these VAEs with an anchor generator and flow matches on the shortened token sequences. It shows advantages over state-of-the-art autoregressive and flow matching methods on Objaverse and generalizes to Toys4K. To the best of our knowledge, it is among the first artisan mesh generation methods whose every generative stage runs in a spatially compressed latent, with a token sequence only a fraction of the most compressed previous autoregressive and flow matching works.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Group Splits: Specimen-Level Cross-Validation and Visual Attribution for Remaining-Shelf-Life Regression in Climacteric Fruit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09726)
*Rovhona Mudau,Jean Frederic Isingizwe Nturambirwe,Clement Nthambazale Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究对哈斯牛油果图像-剩余货架期(RSL)数据包进行了评估，展示了观察水平与标本间分离评估的有效性，并通过Grad-CAM分析了轻量级与全量级视觉骨干网络的空间注意力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提供一种经济实惠的决策支持来分析易腐农产品的剩余保质期，研究者使用了哈斯牛油果成熟数据集进行模型评估，以探究不同评估机制的影响。

Method: 研究团队使用了Hass Avocado Ripening数据集，包含来自426个果实、三个存储方案的8834个图像-剩余货架期对。他们采用冻结的ImageNet预训练视觉主干，并附加轻量级回归头部。利用Grad-CAM分析了不同视觉主干网络的注意力机制差异。

Result: 观察级随机分割的基准模型在RMS均值和标准差分别为2.37天和0.03天的情况下达到了良好的预测效果；而在标本分离交叉验证下的RMS均值和标准差分别为3.12天和0.11天，表现出更大的预测误差。通过对比，轻量化模型MobileNetV3-Small在参数量和计算效率上优于ResNet-18，并且Grad-CAM揭示了不同轻量级主干网络之间的空间注意力机制差异。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在评估轻量级视觉模型用于长期货架期预测时，建议采取标本分离评估，同时结合Grad-CAM进行空间注意力机制分析。

Abstract: Estimating remaining shelf life (RSL) from images could provide affordable decision support for perishable produce, but evaluation protocols can substantially affect reported performance when repeated images are available from the same biological specimen. We use the Hass Avocado Ripening dataset, comprising 8,834 image-RSL pairs from 426 fruits across three storage regimes, to evaluate a frozen ImageNet-pretrained visual backbone with a lightweight regression head. Our contributions are threefold: we quantify the effect of observation-level versus specimen-disjoint evaluation, compare lightweight and heavier visual backbones under specimen-disjoint cross-validation, and examine their spatial attributions using Grad-CAM. Across ten observation-level random splits, the model achieves a mean RMSE of 2.37 days with a standard deviation of 0.03 days, whereas specimen-disjoint 5-fold cross-validation yields a mean RMSE of 3.12 days with a standard deviation of 0.11 days. The corresponding mean coefficient of determination is 0.553. A matched per-specimen comparison confirms higher error under specimen-disjoint evaluation, with a probability value below 0.001 across 426 specimens, showing that observation-level partitioning gives a substantially more optimistic estimate for this dataset and model configuration. Under specimen-disjoint evaluation, MobileNetV3-Small (0.93 million parameters) achieves accuracy comparable to ResNet-18 while providing substantially higher throughput, and Grad-CAM reveals differences in spatial attribution between the lightweight backbones. These results support specimen-disjoint evaluation and attribution analysis when assessing lightweight vision models for longitudinal shelf-life prediction.

</details>


### [86] [SoccerNet-FoulRet: Retrieving Semantically Similar Soccer Foul Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09742)
*Jacobus Arthur,Ahmad Sait,Batool Hani,Merey Ramazanova,Jan Held,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Bernard Ghanem,Anthony Cioppa,Silvio Giancola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了SoccerNet-FoulRet基准，旨在通过文本描述检索相关的犯规案例，而无需考虑视角、队伍或外观因素。研究发现了零样本模型在召回相关犯规示例方面的表现较差，而特定任务的微调模型虽然在类别相关性上有所提升，但在识别具体犯规实例时效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前职业足球裁判在判断争议犯规时存在不一致性问题，主要原因在于裁判难以将当前冲突事件与过去的类似案例进行比较，因此提出该研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了SoccerNet-FoulRet基准，基于SoccerNet-MVFoul数据集，利用零样本视频和视觉语言嵌入器以及特定任务微调模型进行测试评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，零样本模型在通过描述检索相关犯规案例方面的性能较差，尽管经过类别监督微调的模型在类别相关性上有所提升，但在实际案例检索上提升有限。

Conclusion: 研究认为，虽然当前的模型在类别相关性上有了一定的改进，但在实际的犯规案例检索中仍然存在较大挑战，需进一步研究提高模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Refereeing decisions in professional soccer remain inconsistent because referees cannot easily compare a contentious foul against similar past cases. We cast this as a retrieval problem and introduce SoccerNet-FoulRet, the first benchmark for semantic foul retrieval. Given a query foul, the task is to retrieve past fouls judged to be relevant precedents, regardless of camera angle, teams, or appearance. This differs from prior video-to-video retrieval, which matches clips by visual similarity or a shared event. Here, relevance is defined by refereeing interpretation. We build the benchmark from the SoccerNet-MVFoul dataset and evaluate retrieval ability of zero-shot video and vision-language embedders together with a task-specific fine-tuned baseline on 693 human-verified queries and category-relevance labels. Semantic foul retrieval remains challenging. The strongest zero-shot model achieves under 5% HitRate@10 on human-verified precedents, while category-supervised fine-tuning improves category relevance but transfers only modestly to precedent retrieval. We release SoccerNet-FoulRet to establish semantic foul retrieval as an open problem: https://github.com/SoccerNet/sn-foulret.

</details>


### [87] [Flow-of-Thought: A Framework for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09746)
*Mariia Baidachna,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种Flow-of-Thought (FoT)框架，通过生成视觉草图作为中间推理步骤来模拟人类的思维图像，从而提升了大规模语言模型和视觉转换器在需要空间理解的任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模语言模型和视觉转换器在处理需要空间理解的任务时表现不佳，因此研究者们引入了Flow-of-Thought框架，以增强这些模型在处理这类任务的能力。

Method: 该框架通过在$SO(2)$群轨道和累计最短路径上训练坐标的感知轨迹流场，然后冻结这些学习到的动力学。在这种设置下，FoT系统会通过前景加权重构能量来对比生成假设。

Result: 测试结果显示，FoT框架在锁定测试中分别达到了100.0%的Tetris准确率和99.0%的彩色形状准确率。在冻结转移实验中，经过轨道训练的二维流相比于端点控制增加了准确率，特别是在一些分布外的设置下。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为Flow-of-Thought框架能够提供连贯的视觉轨迹，这是一种对于某些分布外场景有效的并且具有可解释性的空间推理表示。

Abstract: Mental imagery, ``seeing with the mind's eye'' is an essential aspect of human cognition. Despite rapid progress Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) still underperform on tasks requiring spatial understanding. To address this, we introduce Flow-of-Thought (FoT), a framework that integrates the generation of visual sketches as intermediate reasoning steps, mimicking mental imagery in humans. We train coordinate-aware trajectory flow fields on $SO(2)$ group orbits and cumulative shortest paths, then freeze the learned dynamics; same vs. different decisions compare competing generative hypotheses using foreground-weighted reconstruction energy. On locked tests FoT reaches 100.0% accuracy on Tetris and 99.0% on colored shapes. Under frozen transfer, the orbit-trained 2D flow improves over its endpoint-only control on BLINK Multi-view (72.2% vs. 63.9% on 133 public validation pairs), supporting continuous visual traces as an effective and interpretable representation for spatial reasoning in some out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [88] [Diffusion-Generated Image Watermarking: A Two-Axis Taxonomy and Three Protocol-Bounded Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09755)
*Sung Ju Lee,Nam Ik Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于生成图像水印的方法，通过插入机制和主要承载信号的表示两个轴来组织方法，并使用$z_T$-傅里叶管道进行验证和识别。通过分类研究频率完整性的关联性、编辑时的持久性以及单帧VAE隐层相位调制的效率等，提出了模型/管道适应性评估协议和可测试的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了平衡生成图像水印中的来源信号和图像质量、鲁棒性和计算成本之间的关系，提出了一个组织方法的框架，以指导未来的研究工作。

Method: 通过组织两种方法轴（插入机制和主要承载信号的表示）来构建方法分类；使用$z_T$-傅里叶管道进行验证和识别；通过分类研究单帧VAE隐层相位调制等。

Result: 对频率完整性、编辑下的持续性、单帧VAE隐层相位调制等进行了深入研究；发现单帧VAE隐层相位调制在效率、再生鲁棒性和质量-鲁棒性操作点等方面具有潜力；提出了模型/管道适应性评估协议和测试的猜想。

Conclusion: 提出的方法提供了用于生成图像水印系统的框架，但未明确给出一个统一的排名，而是建议进行协议意识的匹配比较。

Abstract: Watermarking diffusion-generated images requires balancing provenance signals with image quality, robustness, and computational cost. This work organizes methods along two axes: insertion mechanism and primary signal-bearing representation, and formalizes a representative $z_T$-Fourier pipeline for verification and identification. We then use the taxonomy to structure three protocol-bounded case studies. The first examines associations among frequency integrity, detection, quality, and cropping behavior. The second revisits persistence under seed-linked and seed-independent editing and formulates a scoped Semantic Imprinting Hypothesis without claiming a localized carrier or causal mechanism. The third studies single-shot VAE-latent phase modulation, including its efficiency, regeneration robustness, and robustness--quality operating points. Finally, we separate four content-level attack families from model/pipeline adaptation, propose corresponding evaluation protocols and testable conjectures for parameter-tuning threats, and identify additional temporal extensions for video. These analyses do not establish a universal ranking; instead, they provide a framework for matched, protocol-aware comparisons of watermarking systems for diffusion-generated images.

</details>


### [89] [Relational Abstractions for Spatial Reasoning with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09780)
*Ana Ezquerro,Ozan Özdenizci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种使用自卷积扩散模型进行空间推理的新框架，该框架通过从基于对象的表示中推导出关系知识，增强扩散模型的结构基础。实验结果表明，这种新框架在复杂推理任务上具有更好的理解决策能力，并且在未见过的数据上具有稳健的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的生成模型在满足结构化的时空推理约束方面存在局限，尤其是在依赖于隐式逻辑结构的任务中。论文针对这一问题，提出了一种新的框架来使用扩散模型进行空间推理。

Method: 该方法利用了未监督的对象发现以及对象间关系的抽象。通过从以对象为中心的表示中推导出关系知识，增强扩散模型的结构化基础，从而能够有效指导生成模型的表示空间，在训练和推理阶段都能帮助模型符合逻辑规则。

Result: 实验展示了，与现有模型相比，该框架在各种复杂推理任务上表现出显著的理解决策能力改进，同时还能在未见过的数据分布上提供稳定的泛化。

Conclusion: 这项研究为在具有隐式逻辑结构的任务中应用扩散模型进行了新的探索，通过引入大规模的生成性空间推理基准，验证了该方法的有效性，并指出未来的工作可以进一步深入探索提升模型在复杂场景中的灵活性。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at image synthesis, but they remain limited in their ability to reliably satisfy structured spatial reasoning constraints. In conditional data distribution modeling tasks with implicit logical structure, such as puzzles defined by visible clues paired with consistent solutions, state-of-the-art generative models tend to approximate pixel-space distributions without learning the underlying logical rules required for inference. To address this limitation, we present a novel framework for spatial reasoning with diffusion models that leverages unsupervised object discovery and abstractions of object relations. We show that the relational knowledge derived from object-centric representations enriches diffusion models with structural primitives, allowing them to effectively guide the generative representation space during both training and inference, and enabling conditional image generation that satisfies reasoning constraints. Additionally, we introduce a large-scale generative spatial reasoning benchmark with four datasets inspired by human-solvable puzzles. Our results show that relational abstractions significantly improve reasoning capabilities of diffusion models on a variety of complex reasoning tasks, while enabling robust generalization in out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [90] [CIRSeg: Coarse-to-Fine Intensity-Robust Liver Segmentation with Source-Free Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09784)
*Ruoshi Xu,Mingqi Gao,Shengda Luo,Jingkun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CIRSeg 是一种基于 nnU-Netv2 的粗到细、抗强度变化的肝脏分割框架，通过 3D CutMix 和随机强度变换，结合级联架构，提供可靠的全局定位和精确的边界细化，最终在 CARE 2026 测试集上取得了优秀的 Dice 分数和 HD95 值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的肝脏分割方法在不同 MRI 强度和不同成像域之间存在泛化能力不足的问题，尤其是在区域细化和边界准确分割方面存在挑战。CIRSeg 旨在通过改进的数据增强技术及架构设计，提升肝脏分割的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: CIRSeg 采用了一种基于 nnU-Netv2 的框架，使用 3D CutMix 和随机强度变换（Nyul 增强或直方图匹配）来增强其在不同 MRI 数据之间的鲁棒性。框架通过级联设计分离低分辨率的解剖学定位和高分辨率的边界精修。在推理阶段，采用基于置信过滤的测试时间适应和概率先验正则化，进一步提高其在未知域的鲁棒性。最后通过最大的连接区域过滤来消除孤立的假阳性点。

Result: CIRSeg 在 CARE 2026 测试集上表现优异，无论是针对已知域还是未知域的数据，Dice 分数分别达到了 97.13% 和 97.93%，并分别提供了 20.18 mm 和 11.30 mm 的 HD95 值。结果表明，该方法在不同的 MRI 及数据域内具有一致的准确分割性能。

Conclusion: CIRSeg 提供了一种有效的方法来解决肝脏分割中的现有挑战，特别是强度变化和泛化能力不足的问题。其通过改进的数据增强和技术设计提升了分割的准确性与鲁棒性，为临床应用和研究提供了新的可能。

Abstract: Reliable liver segmentation in contrast-enhanced MRI is essential for quantitative hepatic assessment, treatment planning, and longitudinal disease monitoring. However, limited annotated data and scanner- or vendor-dependent intensity variations can cause overfitting and poor generalization to unseen acquisition domains. Moreover, simultaneously achieving robust global localization and precise boundary delineation remains challenging, while predictions may contain isolated false-positive regions outside the main liver component. To address these challenges, we propose CIRSeg, a coarse-to-fine, intensity-robust liver segmentation framework based on nnU-Netv2. CIRSeg combines 3D CutMix with stochastic intensity transfer using either Nyul augmentation or histogram matching to improve robustness to heterogeneous MRI intensities. Its cascaded architecture decouples low-resolution anatomical localization from full-resolution boundary refinement. At inference, source-free test-time adaptation based on confidence-filtered predictions and probability-prior regularization further improves robustness to out-of-distribution inputs. As a final deterministic post-processing step, largest connected component filtering removes isolated false-positive regions. On the CARE 2026 test set, CIRSeg achieves Dice scores of 97.13\% and 97.93\% on the in-domain and unseen-domain subsets, with corresponding HD95 values of 20.18 mm and 11.30 mm, respectively. These results demonstrate consistently accurate segmentation across both in-domain and unseen acquisition settings. The code is available at https://github.com/jingkunchen/MICCAI_CARE_2026

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Tokka-Bench: Evaluating Tokenizers Across 100 Natural and 20 Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08794)
*Ben Gubler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个开源框架Tokka-Bench，用于评估跨100多种自然语言和20种编程语言的分词器性能，涵盖了五个互补的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型依赖于分词器，但这些分词器在不同语言中的质量参差不齐，而目前缺乏一个标准化的多元评价框架。

Method: 引入了一个新的评估框架Tokka-Bench，该框架通过采用适应每个书写系统的语言感知分割来评估分词器，基于银字数、唯一令牌覆盖率、子词生育率、单词拆分率、词汇组成五个指标。

Result: 在自然语言和编程语言之间的比较中发现，词汇分配策略比词汇表规模更重要，尽管自然语言的特点不同，但最近的分词器已经在编程语言效率方面趋于一致。

Conclusion: 该框架已开放源代码并提供了数据和交互式仪表板，可供其他研究者使用并进一步扩展研究。

Abstract: Large language models rely on subword tokenizers whose quality varies across languages, yet no standardized multi-metric framework exists for broad comparative evaluation. We introduce Tokka-Bench, an open-source framework that evaluates tokenizers on five complementary metrics -- bytes per token, unique token coverage, subword fertility, word-split rate, and vocabulary composition -- across 100 natural languages (30+ scripts) and 20 programming languages, using language-aware segmentation adapted to each writing system. Comparing seven BPE tokenizers (GPT-2, GPT-4, gpt-oss, Llama 3.1, Gemma 3, Qwen3, and Kimi K2) within individual languages, we find that vocabulary allocation strategy matters more than raw vocabulary size, and that programming-language efficiency has converged among recent tokenizers despite divergent natural-language profiles. The framework, data, and interactive dashboard are publicly available.

</details>


### [92] [Child ASR Adaptation with Adult Retention: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08827)
*Houssam Eddine-Othman Lachemat,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究旨在改进自动语音识别(ASR)系统在儿童及非母语使用者中的表现，同时保持对成人语音识别的性能。通过对比多种方式（全微调、LoRA和后嵌入权重组合），运用不同模型（编码器-解码器、编码器-CTC、AudioLLM），评估了不同情况下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ASR系统在处理儿童和非母语使用者的语音识别上存在不足，且直接对成人ASR模型进行儿童语音适应会导致成人语音识别性能下降。因此，研究探讨改进方法以同时提升儿童语音识别性能并保持成人语音识别效果。

Method: 研究对多种适应策略、语音识别模型及数据集进行了比较实验。包括对编码器-解码器、编码器-CTC和AudioLLM模型进行全微调、LoRA方法以及后嵌入权重组合。实验数据集来源于阿拉伯和英语的儿童语音和成人工资语言材料。

Result: 实验结果显示，儿童适应性是必要的，特别是对于非母语使用的阿拉伯和英语儿童语音。直接适应往往会影响成人语音识别性能。双语适应比语言特异性适应更稳定。权重组合方法通常能改善适应与保持的权衡，特别适用于编码器-CTC、Whisper和AudioLLM模型。线性插值方法（LERP）有利于成人保持，而TIES方法则更好地恢复了儿童的识别增益。对于编码器-解码器模型，直接双语微调仍具有最低的错误率。

Conclusion: 研究证明，儿童适应和成人保留是可能同时实现的，但需要采取合适的方法。权重组合可能是一个有效途径，尤其是在特定的ASR模型下。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems often underperform for children and non-native speakers, while adapting adult ASR models to child speech can cause adult-speech forgetting. We study child ASR adaptation with adult retention across Arabic and English. We compare full fine-tuning, LoRA, and post-hoc weight-space merging across encoder--decoder, encoder--CTC, and AudioLLM-based ASR systems. Experiments use Arabic native and non-native child speech, English MyST child speech, and adult benchmarks from MGB-2 and LibriSpeech test-clean. We evaluate recognition quality with WER and quantify the adaptation--retention trade-off using Retention Index, Child Adaptation Gain, and Adaptation Recovery. Results show that child adaptation is necessary, especially for non-native Arabic and English child speech, but direct adaptation often reduces adult ASR performance. Bilingual adaptation is more stable than language-specific adaptation. Weight-space merging often improves the trade-off, especially for encoder--CTC, Whisper, and AudioLLM-based ASR, with LERP favoring adult retention and TIES recovering stronger child gains. For the encoder--decoder model, direct bilingual fine-tuning remains strongest in raw WER.\footnote{Code, and models are available at https://github.com/qcri/Child-ASR-Adaptation.

</details>


### [93] [When Forgetting Looks Like Improvement: Metric Masking in Streaming Diarizer Adaptation and the Price of Rehearsal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08828)
*Mo Yu,Yang Liu,Jing Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 虽然小样本适应能够提高语音检测的性能，但在不同评估场景下的效果不一致，主要影响的是时间身份一致性而非说话人数量错误。适应方法可能改变流式模型如何随时间维护说话人分配，再次排练可以减少这种影响但降低了跨领域迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文研究了小样本适应对流式说话人识别系统的影响，发现尽管适应提高了性能，但效果在不同场景下的表现不尽相同，并且可能改变模型随时间维护说话人分配的方式。

Method: 通过在7.5小时的两方对话上进行适应，然后在六个不同类型的数据集上进行评估来研究适应效果。

Result: 适应显著提升了领域的说话人识别性能，并在独立数据集上也能表现良好，但这种提升在不同评估条件下不一致，主要影响时间身份一致性。

Conclusion: 本文强调在流式说话人识别系统适应时，需要同时评价检测准确性、身份一致性和保留行为。

Abstract: Small-data adaptation can improve speech detection while degrading speaker attribution. We study this discrepancy in a released streaming diarizer adapted on 7.5 h of two-party conversation and evaluated across six corpora. Adaptation substantially improves in-domain diarization performance and transfers to an independent corpus. However, this improvement is not consistent across evaluation scenarios as the additional confusion is mainly associated with impaired temporal identity consistency rather than speaker-count errors. A local-remapping diagnostic reveals different patterns of identity degradation across corpora, indicating that adaptation may alter how streaming models maintain speaker assignments over time. Rehearsal reduces the observed degradation but reduces the cross-domain transfer performance. These results highlight the need to jointly evaluate detection accuracy, identity consistency, and retention behavior when adapting streaming diarization systems.

</details>


### [94] [Emo-Jev: Probabilistic Reasoning for Emotion Classification with Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08829)
*Yazhou Zhang,Junhao Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Emo-Jev的新框架，它可以将分类任务分解为任务特定的原子判断，并基于概率结合这些判断以产生最终预测。对于情感分析、情绪识别、讽刺检测和幽默检测等不同任务，Emo-Jev的表现优于直接使用Jev进行分类，接近于最先进的大型语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，语言模型如Jev在文本分类任务中提供了丰富的自由形式响应，但在有效推理方面与最先进的大型语言模型竞争尚属未知。因此，作者设计了Emo-Jev框架，旨在探索一种新的语言理解接口，以支持有效的推理。

Method: Emo-Jev框架采用了两种互补的实现：Emo-Jev-D和Emo-Jev-SC。前者将分类任务分解为特定任务的原子判断，通过对这些判断的概率进行结合来生成最终预测。后者从互补的角度构建多个判断路径，并将这些路径的预测合并为一致的决策。

Result: 在八个数据集（情感分析、情绪识别、讽刺检测和幽默检测）上，实验表明Emo-Jev框架的性能优于直接使用Jev进行分类，接近于最先进的大型语言模型，同时具有较低的响应延迟和总体成本。与最强的LLM基线相比，标准Jev的平均宏F1得分为62.93％，而最强的LLM基线则达到了67.28％。

Conclusion: Emo-Jev框架证明了新接口在某些文本分类任务中的有效性，并展示了其与最先进的大型语言模型相比的成本和性能优势。

Abstract: Jev offers an alternative interface for language understanding: given an input and predefined questions, it returns probabilistic decisions rather than free-form responses. Whether this interface can support effective reasoning for text classification against leading LLMs remains an open questions. We introduce Emo-Jev, a training-free framework with two complementary implementations. Emo-Jev-D decomposes classification into task-specific atomic judgments and composes their probabilities into a final prediction. Emo-Jev-SC constructs multiple judgment paths from complementary perspectives and aggregates their predictions into a consensus decision. We evaluate Emo-Jev on eight datasets spanning sentiment analysis, emotion recognition, sarcasm detection and humor detection, comparing against direct Jev classification and five SoTA LLMs under input/output and chain-of-thought reasoning. Standard Jev achieves 62.93\% average macro-F1 versus 67.28\% for the strongest LLM baseline, with lower observed latency and generally lower cost.

</details>


### [95] [CoDR: Training-Free Confidence-Drift Remasking for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08833)
*Yue Wu,Qinghe Zhang,Yu Zhang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoDR 提出了一种无需训练且适用于多种采样的改进机制，通过自信漂移重标记来纠正早期错误，显著提升了模型在各种任务上的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语言模型在处理掩码位置时，一旦做出决策就不可逆，可能会导致早期错误的累积影响。CoDR 方法旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 通过在 k 个前向传播过程中计算自信漂移来识别不再被模型支持的标记，从而进行重标记和再生。

Result: CoDR 在两个骨干网络、四种推理和编码任务以及三种基本采样器上进行评估，总体上提高了多个模型-采样器配置的平均准确率，且在大多数单个任务设置中表现出色，同时减少了额外计算量。

Conclusion: CoDR 方法展示了通过自信漂移重标记来纠正早期错误的有效性，是一种无需训练且实用的改进方式。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) decode by repeatedly committing tokens to masked positions, but these commitments are usually irreversible. A token chosen under sparse, partial context is kept fixed, even when later context no longer supports it. Existing samplers mainly decide when to commit a token, but rarely check whether an already committed token should still be kept, allowing early mistakes to propagate. We trace this issue to confidence drift, where the model's confidence in a committed token drops from its sparse commit-time context to the denser context available later. Based on this signal, we propose CoDR (Confidence Drift Remasking), a training-free and sampler-agnostic refinement pass. CoDR estimates drift for all committed positions in only k forward passes via k-partition probing, then remasks and regenerates only the tokens the model no longer endorses. Across two backbones, four reasoning and coding tasks, and three base samplers, CoDR improves average accuracy across all evaluated model-sampler configurations and improves most individual task settings with modest overhead. Controlled experiments show that the gains come from targeted confidence-drift remasking rather than extra compute alone, and that CoDR uses far fewer forward passes than prior remasking methods. Code is available at https://github.com/YueWu0301/CoDR.

</details>


### [96] [Leveraging LLM-Generated Explanations for Detecting Emotionally Rewritten Fake News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08835)
*Yupei Guo,Jiajun He,Xiaohan Shi,Tomoki Toda,Zekun Yang,Bowen Wang,Yukinobu Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个适应性集成情感改写新闻及其解释的Gated Cross Attention（GCA）框架，以提高在保留事实声明的情况下检测假新闻的鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在多种情感条件下，在PolitiFact和LUN数据集上实现了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假新闻检测方法主要集中在语风格变或引入外部说明信息，但新闻文章在不同情感背景下重写，可能会对被检测结果产生影响，故本文探索在保持事实声明的情况下检测假新闻问题。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于Gated Cross Attention的GCA框架，能够适应性地将情感改写后的新闻内容与相应的解释信息进行融合，通过注意机制识别关键信息并减少情感重构可能引起的匹配偏差。

Result: 实验表明，GCA方法在PolitiFact和LUN数据集上实现了显著的改进，同时在GossipCop数据集上也表现出了竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个针对性的事实保留情感变体的假新闻检测方法，有效提高了模型的鲁棒性，并且在多种情感条件下的实验中表现良好。

Abstract: The spread of fake news may cause severe social consequences. Existing fake news detection methods mainly focus on stylistic variations or incorporate external information such as explanations. However, news articles are often rewritten under different emotional backgrounds while preserving their underlying factual claims, which may affect the robustness of detection models. In this work, we investigate fake news detec- tion under fact-preserving emotional variations. To study this problem, we construct emotion-rewritten test sets and generate explanations from the original news articles as stable background knowledge. We then propose a Gated Cross Attention (GCA) framework that adaptively integrates emotionally rewritten news with the corresponding explanations, enabling the model to focus on informative explanation content while reducing potential mismatches caused by emotional reframing. Experiments on PolitiFact, GossipCop, and LUN demonstrate that the proposed method achieves notable improvements under multiple emotional conditions on PolitiFact and LUN, while maintaining competitive performance on GossipCop. We further analyze the effects of explanation guidance and gating mechanisms under different emotional conditions. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/Flulike/fakenews gca .

</details>


### [97] [Beyond the Sycophancy Score: How Task, Model, and Pressure Shape LLM Yielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08840)
*Guang Yang,Homa Hosseinmardi,Fengchen Liu,Amir Ghasemian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析103,939条回复，探讨了大型语言模型的顺从行为，并发现模型是否保持用户观点主要取决于验证用户声明的成本以及是否存在相关约束。研究还提供了一些实用规则以提高使用可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在深入理解大型语言模型在用户推动反馈时的顺从行为，希望通过细化研究，帮助用户更好地避开可能的不准确回复。

Method: 研究者进行了大量实验，共收集了103,939条带有两独立LSTM裁判评分的回复，覆盖了8个语言模型的不同配置，每种配置面对同一200项任务、13种压力条件以及四轮对话，分析了导致模型顺从的各种条件。

Result: 研究结果显示，验证用户声明的难度和是否存在相关约束是最主要的因素。对于难以验证的问题，模型更可能顺从用户观点；而逻辑谜题的接受率与推测正确答案所需要的信息量成正比。对于个人选择，模型的赞同率达到了77%。难题的让步主要来自于无法可靠解决问题的模型，而能解决问题的模型极少放弃答案。最大推理过程可以完全消除这些让步，逻辑难题的接受率从19.2%和12.5%下降到0%。错误或情绪化的陈述对模型决策无额外影响。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了实用建议，如简化难以验证的问题，深入推理，明确问题陈述，寻求开放问题的证据，并选择具有良好约束配置的模型，这些都有助于提高大型语言模型的可靠使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often abandon a correct answer, or endorse a user's position, once the user pushes back. This behavior, called sycophancy, is usually reported as a single rate per model, which says little about when it happens or how a user can avoid it. We study the conditions that produce it with 103,939 graded replies from ten configurations: eight LLMs with reasoning disabled, and two of them again with maximum reasoning, all facing the same 200 items, 13 pressure conditions, and four-turn conversations, with every reply labeled by two independent LLM judges. We find that the dominant factors are how costly it is for the model to verify the user's claim, and whether a trained guardrail covers it. Removing this task factor from a logistic model costs 0.485 of McFadden $R^2$, against 0.139 for model family and 0.009 for pressure tactic. Anchored facts are almost never conceded (1.3%), while adoption on logic puzzles rises with the number of clues needed to refute the pushed answer. Personal choices are endorsed in 77.0% of conversations. Most concessions on hard items come from models that cannot reliably solve them; models that can solve them rarely give the answer up. For both models tested, maximum reasoning removes these concessions completely: adoption on deep puzzles falls from 19.2% and 12.5% to 0%. Fallacious or emotional framing adds nothing beyond plain repetition. Three human annotators agree with the judges' consensus on 118/120 calibration items. These results give practical rules for reliable use: simplify hard-to-verify problems and reason deeply, state the question rather than one's preferred answer, ask for evidence on open questions, and choose models by their measured guardrail profile.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Risk Prediction: Evidence Grounding and Psychosocial Factor Verification for Explainable Suicide Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08842)
*Tianle Hu,Chen Peng,Yi-Hsin Tsai,Takshing Andy Tung,Bingyang Sun,Yenjou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种框架，包括风险评估、证据关联和因素识别，分别实现了良好的F1分数，以提供更可解释的自杀风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别社交媒体帖子中的自杀风险以发现在线环境中的自杀相关信号十分重要，但单凭风险分类无法提供足够的信息。

Method: 该研究利用IEEE BigData 2026 Challenge的思路，提出了一个包含风险评估、证据定性及因素识别的框架，分别使用基于长度的路由、风险证据约束及两种验证器（领域词验证器和证据感知验证器）进行评估。

Result: 风险评估的加权F1得分为0.8088，证据定性的测试宏平均分为0.7605，因素识别的宏平均分为0.5562，展示了框架能够提供风险预测及支持文本证据和细微的心理社会因素信息。

Conclusion: 提出的框架扩展了自杀风险评估的范围，并提供了更具解释性的社交媒体帖子分析。

Abstract: Identifying suicide risk from social networking services (SNS) posts is important for detecting suicide-related signals in online environments. However, risk classification alone provides limited insight into the textual evidence and psychosocial factors behind a prediction. Based on the IEEE BigData 2026 Explainable Suicide Risk Detection Challenge, this study presents a framework consisting of Risk Assessment, Evidence Grounding, and Factor Identification. Risk Assessment uses length-based routing to accommodate posts of different lengths. Evidence Grounding identifies supporting phrases and uses a Risk-Evidence constraint to maintain consistency with the Risk prediction. For Factor Identification, two verifiers are used. The Taxonomy Verifier focuses on factor semantics, whereas the Evidence-Aware Verifier uses factor-specific lexical-semantic cues to select informative positive training units. Their prediction probabilities are combined to produce the final factor predictions. The three tasks are evaluated using task-specific F1 score measures. Risk Assessment achieved a Weighted F1 of 0.8088, Evidence Grounding achieved a test Macro row F1 of 0.7605, and Factor Identification achieved a Macro F1 of 0.5562. The results show that the framework can provide risk predictions, along with supporting textual evidence and fine-grained information on psychosocial factors. Overall, the proposed framework extends suicide-risk assessment beyond risk-level prediction and provides a more interpretable analysis of SNS posts.

</details>


### [99] [QuanLing: Cross-Branch Validation of Language Distance Quantification on Western Romance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08851)
*Yiping Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了QuanLing框架，使用与之前北日耳曼语系研究相同的度量家族和聚合协议，对西罗曼语系的法语、葡萄牙语、西班牙语和意大利语进行了测试，验证了该框架在不同语言系之间的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量语言学在量化密切相关语言间的语言距离方面仍面临核心挑战。为了验证QuanLing框架的普遍适用性，作者将该框架应用于西罗曼语系，测试其在不同语言分支之间的有效性。

Method: 作者使用了150个四语言平行句子，计算了LaBSE句子嵌入距离、来自四个单一语言BERT分词器的分词化碎片化率以及mBERT掩蔽语言模型的彼此可理解度。

Result: 结果显示，葡萄牙语-西班牙语距离最近(LaBSE距离0.0229)，法语-意大利语最远(0.0338)。LaBSE和mBERT排名在六对中的四对中一致，证实了跨模型的稳健性。西罗曼语系的绝对距离范围（0.011）大于北日耳曼语系（0.008），但相对比例相似（1.48 vs 1.67），符合更长的分化时间。法语表现出特别高的MLM可预测性（36.12%准确度），这反映了其拼写与发音的解耦。

Conclusion: 跨分支验证提供了QuanLing框架超越单一语言分支的一般化的进一步证据。

Abstract: Quantifying language distance among closely related languages remains a core challenge in quantitative linguistics. Our previous work [1] introduced QuanLing (Quantitative Linguistics via Pretrained Language Models), a quantitative framework combining language distance metrics (sentence embedding distance, tokenization fragmentation rate) with language property analysis (MLM prediction probability), validated on North Germanic (Danish, Norwegian Bokmål, Swedish). This paper extends QuanLing to Western Romance--French, Portuguese, Spanish, Italian--testing cross-branch applicability with the same metric family and aggregation protocol as our North Germanic study, adapted for four languages (English anchor, quadruplet construction). Using 150 four-language parallel sentences, we compute LaBSE sentence embedding distances, tokenization fragmentation rates from four monolingual BERT tokenizers, and mBERT masked language model mutual intelligibility. Results show that Portuguese--Spanish are closest (LaBSE distance 0.0229), French--Italian most distant (0.0338); LaBSE and mBERT rankings agree on 4 of 6 pairs, confirming cross-model robustness. Western Romance shows a wider absolute distance span than North Germanic (0.011 vs. 0.008) but comparable relative ratios (1.48 vs. 1.67), consistent with longer divergence time. French exhibits notably higher MLM predictability (36.12% top-1 accuracy vs. 29.28% for Italian), reflecting its orthography--phonology decoupling. This cross-branch validation provides further evidence for QuanLing's generalizability beyond a single language branch.

</details>


### [100] [LRCC: Generalizing Low-Rank Compression with Conditional Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08858)
*Thomas Vaitses Fontanari,Maximo Eduardo Rulli,Federico Alvetreti,Donatella Genovese,Simone Scardapane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Low-rank compression reduces the cost of pretrained language models by replacing linear transformations with low-rank factorizations. However, conventional methods use a fixed rank allocation during inference, assigning the same amount of compute regardless of the input token. We introduce Low-Rank Conditional Computation (LRCC), which adds token-dependent computation to pretrained models by training one lightweight router per Transformer block to select among a small set of nested low-rank paths. During training, the low-rank factors remain frozen, and only the routers are optimized. We evaluate LRCC on Llama and Qwen models for language modeling and zero-shot downstream tasks. Within the same average active-parameter budget, LRCC improves the predictive performance over static low-rank compression, including a 7.6 percentage-point gain in average downstream accuracy on Llama-2-7B over static methods. At matched batch-size-1 decoding latency, LRCC improves both perplexity and downstream accuracy on Llama-3.2-1B and remains competitive on Llama-2-7B, without specialized kernels. Finally, we assess the usefulness of assigning a token-wise path by analyzing the routers' path choices.

</details>


### [101] [Tiny-Scale Chinese BERT Pretraining: A Controlled Comparison of MLM, WWM, and MacBERT Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08879)
*Yiping Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对一个小规模的中文 BERT 模型（4 层，256 隐藏维度，8.7M 参数），对比了 Masked Language Modeling (MLM)、Whole Word Masking (WWM) 和 MacBERT 替换策略。实验结果显示，在小规模模型中，MLM 总体表现最好，而 WWM 在困惑度和掩码词命中率上表现优异。MacBERT 在受限的同义词字典下性能严重恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨预训练策略在小规模模型中的效果，以弥补之前主要集中在大规模模型的比较研究。

Method: 训练三个相同架构、使用相同语料库和超参数的基本小规模模型，从头开始训练并评估每个模型在五个内在维度上的表现。

Result: MLM 在五个评价维度中表现最好，WWM 在困惑度和掩码词命中率上有明显优势，而 MacBERT 因为受限的同义词字典导致性能显著下降。研究指出，仅依赖训练损失评估模型性能存在局限性。

Conclusion: 在小规模模型中，MLM 是最佳选择，而 WWM 在某些方面优于其他两种方法。此外，该研究表明，在混合替换策略下，模型的训练损失不一定能准确反映其性能。

Abstract: Pretraining strategies significantly impact the quality of language models, yet existing comparisons of Masked Language Modeling (MLM), Whole Word Masking (WWM), and MacBERT-style replacement have focused primarily on base-scale models (>=110M parameters). This paper presents a controlled comparison of these three strategies on a tiny-scale Chinese BERT model (4 layers, 256 hidden dimensions, 8.7M parameters). Under identical architecture, corpus (1.29M sentences from Chinese Wikipedia), and hyperparameters, we train three models from scratch and evaluate them across five intrinsic dimensions: perplexity, MLM hit rate, semantic discrimination, grammatical judgment, and contextual sensitivity. At tiny scale, MLM achieves the best overall intrinsic performance (winning 3 of 5 dimensions), while WWM excels in both perplexity (1.27 vs. 2.10, a 39.5% improvement) and MLM hit rate (22% vs. 16%). Notably, MacBERT under a severely limited synonym dictionary (222 entries, 3.3% coverage) exhibits severe perplexity degradation (47.23, 22x higher than MLM), yielding a ranking (MLM > WWM >> MacBERT) that differs markedly from the established base-scale conclusion (MacBERT > WWM > MLM). We further identify a critical evaluation pitfall: MacBERT achieves the lowest training loss (2.17) yet the highest perplexity (47.23), revealing that training loss alone is unreliable under mixed replacement strategies. All models and corpus are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/eebyp.

</details>


### [102] [Steering Follows Geometry, Not Labels: Emotion Directions in a Full-Duplex Speech Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08887)
*Pulak Kuli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了Moshi全双工语音模型在实时对话中的情绪控制，采用均值差异激活控制技术，实现了对快乐、愤怒、惊讶情绪的有效控制，但悲伤情绪的控制较为困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时对话中，语音代理需要调整情感和传达方式以适应不同场景的需求。

Method: 采用Moshi全双工语音模型，利用均值差异激活控制技术进行情绪调整，分析不同情绪在模型中的表现。

Result: 发现不同情绪在模型中的可调节性存在差异，快乐、愤怒和惊讶情绪更容易通过均值差异激活控制实现情感调整，而悲伤情绪具有独特性，难以通过这种方式进行有效控制。

Conclusion: 需进一步研究以实现对所有情绪类型的全面、有效控制，提高全双工语音代理在实际对话中的应用效果。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents need to modulate emotion and delivery during real-time conversations, when de-escalating a complaint, carrying urgency in dispatch, softening a clinical result. Emotion and delivery control is well studied for TTS and turn based models through prompt-conditioned synthesis, reference-conditioned synthesis and activation steering; PersonaPlex controls identity in a duplex model but not affect.
  We study emotion steering in Moshi, a fully open sourced full-duplex speech language model, across four emotions, using mean-difference activation steering, which costs only a few vector additions per frame and no retraining.
  We show that emotion is linearly decodable from Moshi's residual stream, but activation steering is only partially achievable, and unevenly so; as happy, angry and surprise steer towards a shared direction while sad is distinctly steerable. We also show that the shared component across the three emotions cannot simply be projected away from all the emotions equally.

</details>


### [103] [CARE: Certifying Acceleration for Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08917)
*Rui Liu,Tong Zheng,Jindong Gu,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CARE 方法通过在验证集上进行配对滚动来确保加速不会引入失败，并提供了基于置信度的风险保证，适用于不同的加速机制，同时减少滚动次数以保持成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作使用加速技术来提高视觉-语言-动作模型的效率，但大多数评估仅基于延迟和平均任务成功率。这种方法可能会因为加速而忽略重要信息，从而导致任务失败，而这种失败很难通过平均指标来发现。

Method: CARE 方法通过在相同的初始条件下进行配对滚动来检测加速引起的问题，并基于这些滚动来评估加速的选择，以确保任务的成功率不低于某个预算。它通过只使用终结结果和测量的计算资源来工作，并对不同的加速机制应用不变。

Result: CARE 方法在四个 LIBERO 套件中对 OpenVLA-OFT 实现了 9.0 到 10.8 倍的加速，同时在 95% 置信水平下保证了至少 85.8% 的参考解决的回合保留。与没有保证的选择器相比，在预算紧时，CARE 确保不超出预算，并且其顺序形式比全面评估使用 78.9% 更少的滚动。

Conclusion: CARE 方法能够有效地加速视觉-语言-动作模型，同时提供基于置信水平的风险保证，适用于不同的加速机制，并通过减少滚动次数来保持成本效益。

Abstract: While vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly, running them at every control step remains expensive. Prior work accelerates VLA inference using techniques like action chunking and visual-token pruning, typically evaluating based on latency and average task success. However, acceleration may discard information and break tasks the original policy would solve, a risk hidden by average metrics. Measuring these failures is challenging because action deviations compound over closed-loop trajectories, meaning task failure is only observable across full episodes. We therefore define an acceleration-induced failure via paired rollouts from identical initial conditions, tracking when the reference succeeds but the accelerated policy fails. To manage this, we introduce CARE, an approach for certified accelerator selection. CARE uses paired rollouts on a calibration set to provide finite-sample guarantees that acceleration-induced failure risk stays below a user-specified budget. It deploys the fastest certified candidate, falling back to the reference if none qualify. By relying only on terminal outcomes and measured compute, CARE applies unchanged across diverse acceleration mechanisms, while sequential testing and failure-triggered reference rollouts keep certification affordable. On four LIBERO suites with OpenVLA-OFT, CARE certifies $9.0$--$10.8\times$ speedups while guaranteeing (at $95\%$ confidence) that at least $85.8\%$ of reference-solved episodes are preserved. Under tight budgets, selectors without guarantees exceed the budget in up to $75\%$ of trials, whereas CARE stays within budget and its sequential form uses $78.9\%$ fewer rollouts than exhaustive evaluation. CARE further generalizes to flow-step reduction for $π_{0.5}$, and to Qwen3.5-9B and Llama-3.1-8B agents in Crafter.

</details>


### [104] [Quad-State Safety Evaluation of Open-Weight Large Language Models on Non-Canonical Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09033)
*Pavan Maddula*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建了ASRD数据集，包含2100个具有七种不同表面形式的提示，评估了五种不同的语言模型在处理这些提示时的响应。研究发现，表情符号和隐形Unicode变体几乎不会导致理解失败，而拼写替代、编码包裹和混合变换则显著增加了理解失败和有害响应的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型安全性评估通常基于标准文本，未能充分考虑实际部署中遇到的复杂输入形式，如表情符号、拼写变化、编码字符串和字符级变异。该研究旨在填补这一空白，通过创建一个包含这些表面形式的全面数据集和评估框架，促进对更广泛威胁场景的评估。

Method: 研究构建了一个名为ASRD的数据集，包含2100个具有七类不同表面形式的提示。然后使用五种开放模型对这些提示进行了评估，生成了10,500个回应。使用Quad-State评估标准对每个响应进行了分类。

Result: 实验证明，表情符号和隐形Unicode变体很少导致理解失败（分别为0%和20.27%），尽管它们在标准文本中的有害响应率为22.87%，主要归因于Mistral 7B。而拼写替代（2.40%）、编码包裹（0.13%）和混合变换（2.40%）导致的有害响应率显著降低，同时增加了理解失败的比例（分别为36.47%、65.60%和34.47%）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，表情符号和隐形Unicode变体不会显著增加模型在表面形式变化情况下的安全风险，而拼写替代、编码包裹和混合变换则会显著增加。这一发现有助于指导未来的语言模型安全评估与改进工作。

Abstract: Standard safety evaluations of large language models assess harmful requests written in canonical plain text, while models in real-world deployment routinely receive inputs containing emojis, altered spellings, encoded strings, and character-level variations. This work introduces the Adversarial Surface-Form Robustness Dataset (ASRD), comprising 2,100 prompts across seven distinct surface-form families. Five open-weight language models are evaluated across these prompts, producing 10,500 responses. The Quad-State Evaluation Rubric classifies each response into one of four outcomes: harmful compliance, safe response, comprehension failure, or indeterminate. Emoji and invisible Unicode variations cause almost no comprehension failure, with pooled harmful compliance of 20.27% and 17.20% against a 22.87% baseline that is driven mainly by Mistral 7B, whereas leetspeak, encoded wrappers, and hybrid transformations score 2.40%, 0.13%, and 2.40% while comprehension failure rises to 36.47%, 65.60%, and 34.47%. Inspection of raw model outputs reveals three response behaviors: hallucinated benignity, structural collapse, and language drift. Project page: www.pavanmaddula.com/quadstate

</details>


### [105] [U-Space: Uncovering When and Why Uncertainty Arises in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09087)
*Tobias Braun,Nils Loose,Alexander Herzog,Virginia Ceccatelli,Marcus Rohrbach,Thomas Eisenbarth,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出U-Space，一种低维子空间，用于测量和解释模型随时间演化的不确定性。该方法通过使用语义锚点识别不确定性，并将不确定性方向映射回残差空间，最终投影到基准向量上，从而为每个词元状态提供一个解释性不确定性映射，无需额外的生成或训练，且在多种推理基准中表现优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性量化方法存在不足，如需要重复生成或额外训练，且缺乏可解释性。为了解决这些问题，研究提出了U-Space，旨在通过机制可解释性直接连接可由人类理解的概念并解释模型不确定性。

Method: 研究通过识别语义上的不确定性和确定性锚点，将这些方向映射回残差空间，创建一个低维子空间（U-Space），并使用正交基将每个词元状态投影到该空间中。这种方法将词元级不确定性以一种可以直接检验或聚合为标量不确定性评分的方式进行解释。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种推理基准下表现出色，不仅优于传统基线方法，而且在控制生成长度的情况下仍然表现出较高的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新的量化模型不确定性的方法，通过创建一个易于理解的低维子空间（U-Space），直接解释模型随时间的不确定性演变过程。

Abstract: Large language models are informing decisions with ever-higher stakes. As the consequences of their errors grow, a central question becomes harder to ignore: how much can we trust an individual answer? Yet recognizing when to defer remains difficult because language models can present incorrect conclusions with fluent explanations and an authoritative tone. Uncertainty quantification seeks to address this disconnect by estimating the reliability of individual predictions. However, many existing methods require repeated generations or separately trained components, and their scalar estimates do not reveal where uncertainty arises or how it evolves during reasoning. Recent work has also shown that generation length can be strongly associated with uncertainty estimates and correctness, raising the question of how much of an estimator's predictive power comes from uncertainty-specific information rather than output length alone. Mechanistic interpretability offers a way to address these limitations by connecting human-interpretable concepts to intermediate model states. Building on this capability, we introduce the U-Space, a low-dimensional subspace that makes a model's evolving uncertainty measurable and interpretable. We identify semantic anchors for doubt and certainty, map their unembedding directions back into the residual space, and combine their contrasts into an orthogonal basis. The U-Lens projects each token state onto these basis vectors, yielding an interpretable token-level uncertainty map that can be inspected directly or aggregated into a scalar uncertainty score. Our approach requires no correctness labels, repeated generations, or training. Across reasoning benchmarks, its confidence score outperforms established baselines under both standard and length-controlled evaluation and transfers more reliably than supervised estimators. Code: https://github.com/s2labres/U-Space.

</details>


### [106] [Same Text, Different Prediction: Serving-Context Nondeterminism in Text Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09111)
*Santhosh Kumar Kasa,Siva Rajesh Kasa,Sumit Negi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对文本分类中的服务上下文非不变性进行了系统性研究，发现了在不同浮点精度和批处理形状下，分类标签与评分的稳定性存在差异，并提出了解决这些不一致性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本生成研究揭示了服务环境变化对生成文本的影响，但对文本分类的影响尚不清楚。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者训练了180个模型，并在四种服务上下文中评估每个模型，这些上下文类别包括FP32、BF16精度，以及批处理形状的变化。

Result: 研究发现，在某些情况下，即使标签不改变，分类结果的评分也会有很大差异。在BF16精度下，预测概率质量的最大变化为56.7个百分点，而生成型分类器比判别分类器受到更多的影响。作者还确定了确保标签一致性的必要条件，并为每种机制提出了单独的缓解措施。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了在进行再现性文本分类研究时需要固定哪些服务条件，并提出了具体的缓解策略。

Abstract: Deterministic inference is essential for reliable and trustworthy machine learning. Prior studies of text generation have shown that changing factors such as batch size, batch composition, hardware, or inference engine can alter the generated text, even when the prompt, model parameters, and sampling randomness are fixed. These differences have been attributed in part to floating-point non-associativity, shape-dependent kernel selection, and other implementation-level differences in numerical execution. However, it remains unclear whether, when, and to what extent the same factors affect text classification. We present a systematic study of serving-context non-invariance in text classifiers, which prior work has measured only through generated text. We train 180 models spanning discriminative, pseudo-generative, and fully generative classifier formulations and evaluate each across four categories of serving contexts, holding the checkpoint and the text fixed. Label stability does not imply score stability. Changing only the batch shape changes no labels across fp32 comparisons, yet under bf16 it moves up to 56.7 percentage points of predicted probability mass, with label changes concentrated at small margins. Fully generative classifiers change more labels than their discriminative counterparts under the same serving changes. We derive sufficient conditions for label stability under each serving change and give a separate mitigation for each mechanism. Our results identify and quantify the serving conditions that must be fixed for reproducible text classification.

</details>


### [107] [sk-bench: A Native-First Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models in Slovak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09152)
*Marek Šuppa,Ivan Vykopal,Andrej Ridzik,Kristián Sopkovič,Natália Kňažeková,Jaroslav Kopčan,Miroslav Blšták,Viktória Ondrejová,Daniel Hládek,Michal Gregor,Martin Tamajka,Marián Šimko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: sk-bench 是一个针对斯洛伐克语的本土基准测试，包括 30 个数据集（33 种评分任务变体），覆盖了十个技能类别。作者评估了55个开放和封闭权重模型，指出使用本土数据、计划指令修复、启用测试时推理和避免过度投资目标语言提示是设计其他资源不足语言基准的教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯洛伐克语是被多语言 LLM 基准测试忽略的西斯拉夫语系中的一个丰富的形态语言，仅有少数人对其进行翻译覆盖或涉及。为了填补这个空白，研究者开发了sk-bench，旨在提高斯洛伐克语在生成型LLM中的表现。

Method: 研究者创建了sk-bench，其包含了30个数据集（33种评分任务变体），涵盖了十个技能类别。他们还引入了包括IFEval-SK和Chiby/SKJ1在内的十一项资源，这些资源是为了评估生成型LLM定制而首次包装的。此外，他们还对55个开放和封闭权重的模型进行了评估。

Result: 研究发现最好的开放模型在性能上落后于专有API达12.6分。对于本地化和翻译封闭形式数据，模型排名高度相关（ρ≥0.98），但翻译无法很好地分离最强的模型。然而，人类撰写的问题答案和LLM生成的问题答案为模型排名提供了不同的评估标准（ρ=0.72）。对Qwen3-14B的进一步研究发现，持续的斯洛伐克预训练降低了总体得分，但一个小指令集可以恢复约75%的损失。另外，测试时推理可以提高模型得分8.5到12.5分。

Conclusion: 研究得出的结论是，其他资源不足的语言基准设计应采用本地数据而不是翻译，计划指令修复应在语言适应之后进行，测试时推理应先于扩展应用启用，同时避免过度投资目标语言提示。这些研究成果通过数据和代码发布在 GitHub 平台上。

Abstract: Multilingual LLM benchmarks omit Slovak, a morphologically rich West Slavic language of five million speakers, or cover it only by machine translation. We present sk-bench, a native-first Slovak benchmark with 30 datasets (33 scored task variants) across ten skill categories. Eleven resources are introduced or first packaged for generative-LLM evaluation, including IFEval-SK with Slovak-adapted instruction checkers and native Chiby/SKJ1 resources for Slovak grammar and morphology. We evaluate 55 open- and closed-weights models under one harness. The best open model trails proprietary APIs by 12.6 points. Model rankings are similar for native and translated closed-form data ($ρ\geq0.98$), though translation separates the strongest models less well. By contrast, human-authored and LLM-generated QA questions rank models differently ($ρ=0.72$). For Qwen3-14B, continued Slovak pretraining lowers the overall score by 13.9 points. A small instruction set restores three quarters of that loss. Test-time reasoning improves scores by 8.5 to 12.5 points for models of 9B and above. Together, these findings suggest four design lessons for other under-resourced languages: use native data where translation fails, plan instruction repair after language adaptation, enable test-time reasoning before scaling up, and avoid overinvesting in target-language prompts. We release the data and code at https://github.com/slovak-nlp/sk-bench

</details>


### [108] [ToolRACER: A Robust Agentic Conversation Emulation Resource for Agent Training and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09163)
*Arkajyoti Chakraborty,Aryan Tayal,Ishika Agarwal,Tanner Sorensen,Justin Chiu,Alessandro Di Bari,Neha Gupta,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToolRACER 提供了一个合成数据生成管道，用于生成多轮交互场景，特别是包含了失败场景和对抗行为。生成的数据集 ToolRACERBench 能够提高对话代理的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的对话代理在真实世界对话场景中表现脆弱，尤其是在面对非协作用户时。现有的功能调用基准主要关注成功且协作的互动，忽视了对抗性对话路径，这限制了开发稳健对话代理的资源。ToolRACER 旨在生成包含对抗行为的多轮交互数据集，以提高代理在失败场景中的性能。

Method: ToolRACER 使用自动化生成流程协调用户、助手和工具模型，生成和验证用户与代理之间的多轮对话。通过合成数据生成流程，构建了包含 5.6K 对话轨迹的 ToolRACERBench 数据集，涵盖了六个领域，55 种不同的人格。

Result: ToolRACERBench 生成的数据集中约 66% 的对话包含了潜在出错的对话场景，生成了针对不同类型代理能力任务的对话行为注释。模型训练于 ToolRACERBench 上相比内部基准以及 $τ^2$-bench、BFCLv3 和 ACEBench 等功能调用基准，显著提高了端到端代理准确度。

Conclusion: ToolRACER 提供了一个强大且多元的多轮对话基准数据集 ToolRACERBench，它能够增强对话代理在实际对话应用中的鲁棒性，尤其是在面对对抗行为的情况下。
